# Screenshot-led complaint workspace, evidence and collaborative mediator UX overhaul

Implementation tasks begin pending. Completion requires behavioral validation evidence.

## CIMP-UX-01 Verify the screenshot baseline through real user and staff flows

- Status: completed
- Completion: validation
- No-change completion: allowed
- Priority: P0
- Goal: UXG-foundation
- Subgoal: UXS-foundation-baseline
- Depends on: 
- Outputs: tests/test_cimp_ux_01.py
- Submodules: 
- Validation: python -m pytest -q --run-llm --run-network --run-heavy tests/test_cimp_ux_01.py
- Acceptance: Run the inherited pinned Chromium/ASGI capability against synthetic encrypted cases; verify actual owner, lawyer, message, and admin routes, not a mockApi or a diagnostic-only mounted component. Bind baseline screenshots to source/runtime/viewport/actor/state and assert real DOM/response semantics and owned cleanup. Characterize the measured stale No case selected/access denied header separately from the genuinely authenticated working inquiry; record that defect rather than falsely asserting baseline UX success. Include desktop and mobile.
- Implementation provider: grok
- Provider role: grok-only
- Model: grok-4.7
- Router: ipfs_accelerate_py.llm_router
- Provider fallback: disabled
- Runtime guidance: Implement this bounded task in the existing complaint-generator architecture and use the screenshot-led experience contract. Reuse the qualified actual workspace, case, evidence, graph, review, message, private-storage and mediator owners; do not create a duplicate client-only interview or database. Development code generation is authorized through ipfs_accelerate_py.llm_router provider grok_cli, model grok-4.7, fallback disabled. This development authorization never consents to case-data egress. Tests use new synthetic data with private HOME/TMP/XDG/orchestration roots, no provider/network/dependency/browser installation, and the actual pinned SDKs. Do not inspect real profiles, sessions, browser state, .env, keys, evidence, exports or unrelated campaigns. Never send real external messages, deploy, push, clone/reset CommonCrawl, recursively update submodules, fabricate statuses/receipts/claims/child births, or reset paid budgets/holds. Preserve the completed intake/mediator/GUI60 specifications, boards, histories and qualification evidence. Keep factual assertions, evidence, admitted law/dependency requirements, AI suggestions, human review, parsed IR and actual proof separate. Maintain actual actor versus canonical encrypted owner, CAS/idempotency, key/consent/source revisions and late-result refusal. Use real pinned Chromium/Node with pipe control and same-origin actual ASGI handlers under inherited denial of non-AF_UNIX sockets; no mockApi, fake auth, diagnostic-only panels or screenshot-only success. Every UI task must include real DOM/interaction assertions and appropriate real backend/state checks, plus honest error/empty/locked/stale states and owned cleanup. Hook work needs genuine operation effects and public workflow entry points, not registry presence, caller flags or manufactured receipts. Missing capability is an explicit unavailable/failure result, never a successful fallback. Write the declared focused pytest module with meaningful positive and negative required cases and zero skips. Keep validation under the existing900-second task budget; never run the old entire132-case matrix inside one worker. Native owns selection, attempt counters, validation, merge and completion. Keep output scope small and declare every source/fixture/registration file needed. Avoid tests that merely repeat constants, inspect marker strings, or assert only JSON shape. Read the current source, predecessor outputs and baseline images/annotated visual brief before implementation; if this route cannot ingest images directly, use the measured visual brief and do not claim the model saw pixels.  Task: Verify the screenshot baseline through real user and staff flows Targets: tests/test_cimp_ux_01.py Required outcomes: - Run the inherited pinned Chromium/ASGI capability against synthetic encrypted cases; verify actual owner, lawyer, message, and admin routes, not a mockApi or a diagnostic-only mounted component. - Bind baseline screenshots to source/runtime/viewport/actor/state and assert real DOM/response semantics and owned cleanup. Characterize the measured stale No case selected/access denied header separately from the genuinely authenticated working inquiry; record that defect rather than falsely asserting baseline UX success. Include desktop and mobile. Read docs/ux/complaint_ux_overhaul/experience_contract.md and baseline/screenshot_manifest.json; preserve the source-bound before/after story.

> - Why/status actions never save an answer, and the UI cannot substitute a fixed generic intake form for the shared adaptive interview.
> Read docs/ux/complaint_ux_overhaul/experience_contract.md and baseline/screenshot_manifest.json; preserve the source-bound before/after story.

- Completion: validation
- No-change completion: allowed
- Priority: P1
- Goal: UXG-grounding
- Subgoal: UXS-grounding-law
- Depends on: CIMP-UX-27, CIMP-UX-21
- Outputs: applications/legal_analysis_api.py, static/complaint_legal.js, tests/test_cimp_ux_28.py
- Submodules: 
- Validation: python -m pytest -q --run-llm --run-network --run-heavy tests/test_cimp_ux_28.py
- Acceptance: A changed authority, date, jurisdiction, consent or evidence version retires stale support and prompts a bounded authorized refresh/review. Keep previous versions/audit history without showing old conclusions as current; refusal does not mutate the encrypted case head.
- Implementation provider: grok
- Provider role: grok-only
- Model: grok-4.7
- Router: ipfs_accelerate_py.llm_router
- Provider fallback: disabled
- Runtime guidance: Implement this bounded task in the existing complaint-generator architecture and use the screenshot-led experience contract. Reuse the qualified actual workspace, case, evidence, graph, review, message, private-storage and mediator owners; do not create a duplicate client-only interview or database. Development code generation is authorized through ipfs_accelerate_py.llm_router provider grok_cli, model grok-4.7, fallback disabled. This development authorization never consents to case-data egress. Tests use new synthetic data with private HOME/TMP/XDG/orchestration roots, no provider/network/dependency/browser installation, and the actual pinned SDKs. Do not inspect real profiles, sessions, browser state, .env, keys, evidence, exports or unrelated campaigns. Never send real external messages, deploy, push, clone/reset CommonCrawl, recursively update submodules, fabricate statuses/receipts/claims/child births, or reset paid budgets/holds. Preserve the completed intake/mediator/GUI60 specifications, boards, histories and qualification evidence. Keep factual assertions, evidence, admitted law/dependency requirements, AI suggestions, human review, parsed IR and actual proof separate. Maintain actual actor versus canonical encrypted owner, CAS/idempotency, key/consent/source revisions and late-result refusal. Use real pinned Chromium/Node with pipe control and same-origin actual ASGI handlers under inherited denial of non-AF_UNIX sockets; no mockApi, fake auth, diagnostic-only panels or screenshot-only success. Every UI task must include real DOM/interaction assertions and appropriate real backend/state checks, plus honest error/empty/locked/stale states and owned cleanup. Hook work needs genuine operation effects and public workflow entry points, not registry presence, caller flags or manufactured receipts. Missing capability is an explicit unavailable/failure result, never a successful fallback. Write the declared focused pytest module with meaningful positive and negative required cases and zero skips. Keep validation under the existing900-second task budget; never run the old entire132-case matrix inside one worker. Native owns selection, attempt counters, validation, merge and completion. Keep output scope small and declare every source/fixture/registration file needed. Avoid tests that merely repeat constants, inspect marker strings, or assert only JSON shape. Read the current source, predecessor outputs and baseline images/annotated visual brief before implementation; if this route cannot ingest images directly, use the measured visual brief and do not claim the model saw pixels.  Task: Surface source refresh, changed context, and support retirement Targets: applications/legal_analysis_api.py, static/complaint_legal.js, tests/test_cimp_ux_28.py Required outcomes: - A changed authority, date, jurisdiction, consent or evidence version retires stale support and prompts a bounded authorized refresh/review. - Keep previous versions/audit history without showing old conclusions as current; refusal does not mutate the encrypted case head. Read docs/ux/complaint_ux_overhaul/experience_contract.md and baseline/screenshot_manifest.json; preserve the source-bound before/after story.

Development code generation is authorized through ipfs_accelerate_py.llm_router provider grok_cli, model grok-4.7, fallback disabled. This development authorization never consents to case-data egress. Tests use new synthetic data with private HOME/TMP/XDG/orchestration roots, no provider/network/dependency/browser installation, and the actual pinned SDKs. Do not inspect real profiles, sessions, browser state, .env, keys, evidence, exports or unrelated campaigns. Never send real external messages, deploy, push, clone/reset CommonCrawl, recursively update submodules, fabricate statuses/receipts/claims/child births, or reset paid budgets/holds. Preserve the completed intake/mediator/GUI60 specifications, boards, histories and qualification evidence. Keep factual assertions, evidence, admitted law/dependency requirements, AI suggestions, human review, parsed IR and actual proof separate. Maintain actual actor versus canonical encrypted owner, CAS/idempotency, key/consent/source revisions and late-result refusal. Use real pinned Chromium/Node with pipe control and same-origin actual ASGI handlers under inherited denial of non-AF_UNIX sockets; no mockApi, fake auth, diagnostic-only panels or screenshot-only success. Every UI task must include real DOM/interaction assertions and appropriate real backend/state checks, plus honest error/empty/locked/stale states and owned cleanup. Hook work needs genuine operation effects and public workflow entry points, not registry presence, caller flags or manufactured receipts. Missing capability is an explicit unavailable/failure result, never a successful fallback. Write the declared focused pytest module with meaningful positive and negative required cases and zero skips. Keep validation under the existing900-second task budget; never run the old entire132-case matrix inside one worker. Native owns selection, attempt counters, validation, merge and completion. Keep output scope small and declare every source/fixture/registration file needed. Avoid tests that merely repeat constants, inspect marker strings, or assert only JSON shape.
